```diff
+import sys,site,pathlib,os
+D=pathlib.Path('work/deps').resolve();site.addsitedir(str(D));sys.path.extend([str(D/'win32'),str(D/'win32/lib')]);os.add_dll_directory(str(D/'pywin32_system32'))
+import win32com.client
+h=win32com.client.DispatchEx('HWPFrame.HwpObject')
+print('HWP automation created',flush=True)
+h.XHwpWindows.Item(0).Visible=False
+ok=h.RegisterModule('FilePathCheckDLL','CodexPaperFilePathModule');print('Module',ok,flush=True)
+if ok:
+ print('Open',h.Open(str(pathlib.Path('work/paper_v2.docx').resolve()),'OOXML','forceopen:true'),flush=True)
+ print('HWP',h.SaveAs(str(pathlib.Path('work/paper_v2.hwp').resolve()),'HWP',''),flush=True)
+ print('PDF',h.SaveAs(str(pathlib.Path('work/hwp_v2.pdf').resolve()),'PDF',''),flush=True)
+h.Quit()

@@ -0,0 +1,57 @@
+import ast,json,pathlib
+P=pathlib.Path
+n=json.loads(P('work/notebook.ipynb').read_text(encoding='utf8'))
+changed=[]
+for i,c in enumerate(n['cells']):
+ if c['cell_type']!='code':continue
+ s=''.join(c['source']);old=s
+ if i==4:
+  s='''# Shared plotting style adapted from the supplied summer conference notebook.
+# Graph labels and legends are fitted to avoid overlap; research computations are unchanged.
+from reference_plot_style import (configure_reference_style, plot_clean_bar as _reference_bar,
+    save_reference_figure, draw_bar, finish_bar, label, draw_reference_dashboard)
+configure_reference_style()
+
+def plot_clean_bar(*args, **kwargs):
+    kwargs.setdefault("out_dir", FIG_DIR)
+    return _reference_bar(*args, **kwargs)
+'''
+ else:
+  if i==55:
+   a=s.index('        # 10 ─ dashboard');b=s.index('    return saved',a)
+   s=s[:a]+'''        # 10: six readable plots; no dense ranking table.
+        fig = draw_reference_dashboard(results, title, diagnose_fit_yolo)
+        savefig("10_dashboard.png")
+'''+s[b:]
+  # Rewrite only savefig call spans, retaining comments and all other source text.
+  tree=ast.parse(s);lines=s.splitlines(keepends=True);offset=[0]
+  for line in lines:offset.append(offset[-1]+len(line.encode('utf8')))
+  edits=[];raw=s.encode('utf8')
+  for node in ast.walk(tree):
+   if isinstance(node,ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func,ast.Attribute) and node.func.attr=='savefig':
+    owner=ast.get_source_segment(s,node.func.value)
+    target='plt.gcf()' if owner=='plt' else owner
+    a=offset[node.func.lineno-1]+node.func.col_offset
+    b=offset[node.func.end_lineno-1]+node.func.end_col_offset
+    edits.append((a,b,('save_reference_figure').encode()))
+    # Add figure as the first argument immediately after the call's open parenthesis.
+    pos=raw.find(b'(',b)+1
+    edits.append((pos,pos,(target+', ').encode()))
+  for a,b,repl in sorted(edits,reverse=True):raw=raw[:a]+repl+raw[b:]
+  s=raw.decode('utf8')
+ if s!=old:
+  ast.parse(s);changed.append(i);c['source']=s.splitlines(keepends=True)
+  c['outputs']=[];c['execution_count']=None
+for c in n['cells']:
+ if c['cell_type']=='code':c['outputs']=[];c['execution_count']=None
+n['cells'].insert(0,{'cell_type':'markdown','metadata':{},'source':[
+ '# 그래프 서식 수정본 · 2026-10-13\n',
+ '그래프는 영어로 표기하고 필터 이름은 짧은 영어 이름으로 2~3줄 가로 배치합니다. 나눔고딕 Bold·흰 배경·색상 체계를 유지하고 겹침 방지를 위해 여백과 크기를 조절합니다.\n',
+ '학습·데이터 분할·평가 지표·저장된 측정값은 변경하지 않았습니다. 전체 실행은 원래 설정대로 학습을 수행할 수 있습니다.\n',
+ '**그래프만 다시 만들 때:** `python render_saved_figures.py --results yolo_filter_figures --output yolo_filter_figures_reference`\n']})
+n['cells'].append({'cell_type':'markdown','metadata':{},'source':['## Conference paper figures: single filters, ordered combinations, and noise\n','These plots use detection mAP and saved results only. They do not train or change the detector.\n']})
+n['cells'].append({'cell_type':'code','metadata':{},'execution_count':None,'outputs':[],'source':[
+ 'from paper_filter_figures import make_paper_filter_figures\n',
+ 'make_paper_filter_figures(FILTER_FIG_DIR, os.path.join(FILTER_FIG_DIR, "paper_filter_comparison"))\n']})
+P('work/ball_ok_ng_yolo_v7_reference.ipynb').write_text(json.dumps(n,ensure_ascii=False,indent=1),encoding='utf8')
+print('changed cells',changed)

@@ -0,0 +1,107 @@
+import ast,csv,hashlib,json,pathlib,shutil,zipfile,statistics
+from collections import defaultdict
+from pypdf import PdfReader
+from docx import Document
+from docx.oxml.ns import qn
+from PIL import Image
+import re
+P=pathlib.Path;W=P('work');O=P('outputs');O.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+figures=list((W/'restyled_english').rglob('*.png'))
+assert len(figures)==99,len(figures)
+for f in figures:
+ info=json.loads(Image.open(f).info['Description'])
+ assert not any(re.search(r'[\uac00-\ud7af\u3131-\u318e]',t) for t in info['visible_text']),f
+ assert all(r['angle']==90 for r in info['filter_label_rotations']),(f,info['filter_label_rotations'])
+old=json.loads((W/'notebook.ipynb').read_text(encoding='utf8'))
+new=json.loads((W/'ball_ok_ng_yolo_v7_reference.ipynb').read_text(encoding='utf8'))
+changed=[]
+for i,(a,b) in enumerate(zip(old['cells'],new['cells'][1:])):
+ if a['source']!=b['source']:changed.append(i)
+ if b['cell_type']=='code':ast.parse(''.join(b['source']))
+assert changed==[4,22,34,46,55,61,72],changed
+for f in ['reference_plot_style.py','render_saved_figures.py']:ast.parse((W/f).read_text(encoding='utf8'))
+rows=list(csv.DictReader((W/'figures/ms_per_image.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig')))
+groups=defaultdict(list)
+for r in rows:groups[(r['condition'],int(r['seed']),r['method'])].append(r)
+assert len(rows)==19500 and len(groups)==39
+for r in csv.DictReader((W/'figures/ms_run_results.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig')):
+ g=groups[(r['condition'],int(r['seed']),r['method'])];assert len(g)==500
+ strict=100*sum(int(x['miss'])==0 and int(x['extra'])==0 for x in g)/len(g)
+ acc=100*sum(int(x['match']) for x in g)/len(g)
+ assert abs(strict-float(r['strict_acc']))<1e-8
+ assert abs(acc-float(r['accuracy']))<1e-8
+pdf=PdfReader(W/'docx_verified/paper.pdf');assert len(pdf.pages)==3
+d=Document(W/'paper.docx');assert len(d.inline_shapes)==2 and not d.tables
+assert d.core_properties.author==''
+for s in d.sections:
+ assert abs(s.page_width.mm-190)<.1 and abs(s.page_height.mm-260)<.1
+ assert abs(s.left_margin.mm-20)<.1 and abs(s.right_margin.mm-20)<.1
+assert len(d._element.xpath('.//w:pBdr'))==0
+doc=O/'추계학술대회_3쪽논문_영문그래프.docx';shutil.copy2(W/'paper.docx',doc)
+pkg=W/'delivery_package_english';pkg.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+for p in ['논문에_사용한_그림','수정_코드/fonts','실험결과_원자료','추가_그래프']:(pkg/p).mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
+for n in [1,2]:shutil.copy2(W/f'restyled_english/paper_figure{n}.png',pkg/f'논문에_사용한_그림/그림{n}.png')
+for f in ['reference_plot_style.py','render_saved_figures.py','ball_ok_ng_yolo_v7_reference.ipynb']:shutil.copy2(W/f,pkg/'수정_코드'/f)
+for f in ['NanumGothic.ttf','NanumGothicBold.ttf']:
+ shutil.copy2(P('fonts')/f,pkg/'수정_코드/fonts'/f)
+shutil.copy2(W/'NanumGothic-LICENSE.txt',pkg/'수정_코드/fonts/LICENSE.txt')
+for f in (W/'figures').glob('*.csv'):shutil.copy2(f,pkg/'실험결과_원자료'/f.name)
+for f in ['results_summary.json','sweep_results.json']:shutil.copy2(W/'figures'/f,pkg/'실험결과_원자료'/f)
+for f in (W/'restyled_english').rglob('*.png'):
+ if f.name.startswith('paper_'):continue
+ dest=pkg/'추가_그래프'/f.relative_to(W/'restyled_english');dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);shutil.copy2(f,dest)
+report={'docx_pages_rendered':3,'embedded_figures':2,'tables':0,'per_image_rows_verified':len(rows),
+ 'runs_verified':len(groups),'notebook_changed_original_cell_indices':changed,
+ 'research_results_modified':False,'training_rerun':False,'figures_rendered':len(list((W/'restyled_english').rglob('*.png'))),
+ 'original_notebook_sha256':hashlib.sha256((W/'notebook.ipynb').read_bytes()).hexdigest()}
+(pkg/'검증결과.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+(pkg/'읽어주세요.txt').write_text('''추계학술대회 논문 및 그래프 수정본
+
+논문: 편집 가능한 DOCX 3쪽, 그림 2개, 표 없음, 저자·소속 공란.
+DOCX를 실제 페이지로 렌더링하여 3쪽과 글자/그림 배치를 확인했습니다.
+한글로 가져오면 프로그램의 조판 차이 때문에 줄바꿈이 달라질 수 있습니다.
+
+본문 서식 (제공된 HWP 양식 기준)
+용지 190×260 mm, 좌우 20 mm, 위아래 17.5 mm, 2단 간격 8 mm.
+국문 제목 휴먼명조 17 pt / 영문 제목 HY견명조 15 pt.
+본문 휴먼명조 10 pt / 절 제목 휴먼고딕 11 pt / 소제목 HY중고딕 11 pt.
```
